Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the holomorphic structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 457. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 526 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to holomorphic theory, we decompose the problem into 457 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 526. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a holomorphic algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 752. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 132/5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the holomorphic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^752 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 132-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
